The client activated the Alder rite with the bound stone. The dedicated server saved pulse 15, resumed the same target, and finished with 53 alder trunks at ground level.
- The muted client activated a remote volcano with supplied offerings, a bound Waystone, and a prepared underground lava reservoir. The dedicated server saved pulse four. After restarting, the finished volcano kept a lava source at its summit, the shaft opened through the original terrain, and the chest still contained its emerald. The client saw the lava in the crater.
- Java 17 data generation, JAR reobfuscation, and all 328 required GameTests passed. New cases cover the two crafting recipes, seven sapling variants, bound terrain targets, missing-lava refunds, legacy stages, and the persistent summit source. Packaged SHA-256 is `ea64711a1d3cd5b94f9df7bbff3f312b00ff77e95a6561aa1ac6344f72775417`.

### Mog and Gulg (2026-11-20)

- Both bosses use their original entity IDs, art, idle sounds, and 400 health. The partner shield permits 0%, 20%, 50%, 80%, or 100% of ordinary damage at the original boundaries of 3, 6, 9, and 16 blocks. Damage is capped at 15 before armor. Mog's bow fires faster at airborne targets. Gulg's melee damage and upward knockback increase as Mog approaches.
- The patron's Nether Star requires five active worshippers and searches for a safe point within eight blocks. Either existing boss within 16 blocks prevents a second pair. The shared high-tier worship path requires fifteen worshippers. It checks for duplicates within 64 blocks and searches for a spawn point within sixteen. The original default of `hobgoblinGodSpawnChance = 10` is a 0.1% chance per five-second pulse. `hobgoblinSpawnWeight` defaults to four and drops by one on plains. Legacy `HobgoblinSpawnChance` had no runtime readers. The entity keeps the original `Invul` timer and saves the statue patron UUID in `WitcheryTarget`.
- Each boss drops one to three Koboldite Nuggets and an enchanted chainmail piece, with a chance to drop its signature equipment. Mog's Quiver lets an ordinary bow fire without arrows, applies Weakness, and increases damage against airborne targets. Gulg's Gurdle increases empty-hand damage and launches the victim. Nearby players wearing the complementary gear receive Resistance II. The Koboldite Helm carries its original recipe and deflects an unprotected player's gaze or weakens a mob looking at the wearer.
- The muted survival client placed its previously bound statue and used a supplied Nether Star next to the prepared group. The server recorded both bosses, fifteen surviving worshippers and one remaining star. At two blocks apart Mog ignored ten magic damage. At five blocks its health fell to 398. After the fixture separated the pair and shortened the fight, the client struck down Mog and the server recorded his Nugget and enchanted boots. With supplied equipment the client fired an ordinary bow without arrows and weakened a Husk. The Gurdle launched a second Husk on a punch, and the Helm weakened a third that was looking at the wearer.
- On the final packaged build, both bosses retained their UUIDs and patron after the patron moved away in spectator mode and the dedicated server restarted. Java 17 data generation, JAR reobfuscation and all 361 required Forge GameTests passed. The four new cases cover shield thresholds, melee lift and arrow speed, saved entrance and patron data, Quiver damage and Weakness, and both bosses' loot. The courtship fixture timed out once without a birth. The passing run kept the fixture parents close for the courtship. Packaged SHA-256 is `3dbcfc0f2399d9bb838fda272aa240c25dc783477ab7be1039f07019a7bdf781`, protocol 68.

### Rites of Broken Earth and Moving Earth (2026-11-21)

- Rite 19 consumes a Brew of Erosion in a small white ring and uses its offering position to select one of the legacy eight headings. The first four steps remain intact. Queasy redirects a repeat dose to Blindness or Nausea. The two head recipes use distinct radii, amplifier limits, and counts according to the action. Drain Magic uses the existing infusion charge and HUD sync. The legacy Blood Magic and Thaumcraft integrations remain for the optional compatibility pass.
- `witchery:potion.diseased` reduces attack damage by 0.5 at amplifier zero and by 1 at higher levels. It can leave a disease cell on supported ground. Fluid contact applies one to four minutes of Diseased to susceptible creatures. The cell-removal roll defaults to one in ten, with zero disabling cleanup. Fluid expiry and Purified Milk explicitly replace the cells with air.
- The muted survival client made all nine variants. Reek preserved Regeneration II and Weakness while stripping Speed I. Wasting and Fullness moved food from 20 to 10 to 16. Duration Boost left 4,677 ticks of Speed at the first sample and added Queasy. The Skull stole Strength and the Creeper Head transferred Weakness II to both neighboring pigs.
- The player drank the Rotten Flesh brew, made a disease cell, cleared the status for the contact test, and acquired it again by walking through the fluid. The dedicated restart retained the contact effect and both unused brews. Milestone tests used a fixed removal setting to isolate Purified Milk IV's removal of Diseased, Weakness IV, and the observed fluid cell. The remaining saved bottle reduced the fixture's 200 infusion charge to 150.
- The disease effect uses vanilla effect packets for tracking clients at application, removal and the start of tracking. The muted Xvfb client showed the original overlay on a pig, saw it clear, and saw it return after moving out of range and back to a pig with the effect already active.
- Java 17 data generation and JAR reobfuscation passed. All 553 required GameTests passed before the overlay sync was added. The final revision passed all 21 focused GameTests. Packaged SHA-256 is `920af77861d5cce7d2148cf92a43483eac986aef65c936ae316a51d07e0bba8c`.

### Cauldron Wool and Dye Colors (2026-11-21)

- Wool uses the sixteen original RGB values and writes a separate `Color` field in the cauldron. It leaves the ordered ingredient list untouched. The bottle, gas, and save paths preserve the selected result, including the old nested fluid `Tag.Color`.
- The twelve dye effects use `witchery:potion.colorful`, the original dye ordinal, and 1,800 ambient ticks. They ignore strength, duration, inversion, and particle modifiers. ### Cauldron Movement and Creature Summons (2026-11-21)

- Breath of the Goddess adds Air Hike at 750 power and two capacity. Player motion is vertical at `0.6 + 0.2 * strength` and clears fall distance. Mobs keep their horizontal motion. Slimeball adds Frog Tongue at 150 power and two capacity. It uses the impact at base strength, an actual caster at higher strength, and the original Jump Boost II for creatures. Block hits also pull nearby nonliving entities.
- Stick adds Knockback at 200 power and two capacity. Impact pushes the affected target away from the hit, while a drink pushes other creatures and items within `3 + strength` blocks. The delivery paths now carry their actual impact coordinates separately from ownership.
- Bat Ball and Owlet's Wing cost 1,000 power and two capacity each. Bat Burst spawns ten bats plus strength on a full hit and one plus strength on a successful glancing roll. Bodega requires an active Owl familiar and spawns temporary attackers. Both skip bat and owl targets. Sunflower's five-capacity, 500-power recipe creates a poisonous toad with `SuicideIn` equal to `60 + 40 * strength`. Its expiry applies Poison within three blocks. Named legacy Double Plant metadata zero becomes Sunflower.
- Bone adds Raising for 2,000 power and four capacity. An ordinary impact raises one bound no-drop undead and can add extra summons when strengthened. A ritual uses one temporary summon and the original Mortal Coil formula. The modern spawn check uses clear block space, so creatures standing at the site can move aside. The fixed Kettle brew continues to use its own terrain behavior.
- Flowing brews sample the six adjacent blocks at one percent per direction when they carry a terrain action. The liquid path subtracts one strength from the action. Seeded GameTests exercised the actual liquid tick and the gas ritual's lower coven strength limit.

Frog Tongue moved the pig toward the caster and drunk Knockback moved its neighbor outside three blocks. Bat Burst created ten marked bats. Bodega used without Owl mastery produced no attackers. A poisonous toad saved 97 ticks at the first sample and poisoned the nearby pig on expiry.

The gas ritual raised a skeleton with 398 ticks of Mortal Coil at the first sample. The skeleton expired. The Xvfb client used master volume zero and OpenAL No Output.

### Cauldron Love, Volatility, Fortune and Resizing (2026-11-21)

- Poppy adds Love for 500 power and four capacity. It clears a mature animal's cooldown and gives 600 ticks of normal love. A zombie starts 600 ticks of courtship with a nearby adult and can finish after the effect expires. Successful birth converts the ordinary parents to Zombie Villagers with their saved ownership and tags and creates a wild baby. Villagers start in a village with occupied points of interest and finish with a courting partner after 300 ticks without requiring food.
- Dead Bush adds Volatility for 1,000 power and four capacity. Named legacy Tall Grass metadata zero and the original Dead Bush name both map to this recipe. The curse lasts 3,600 ticks and is incurable. An explosion always triggers it. Ordinary qualifying damage has the original one-in-five to one-in-two chance. Drowning, suffocation, falling, void damage and starvation are excluded. The blast can damage terrain when `allowVolatilityPotionBlockDamage` is enabled and the player's location allows breaking.
- Clay Ball gives Fortune for 1,000 power and six capacity. The effect adds one Fortune level to an ephemeral loot tool or two at amplifier three and above. Barehanded loot uses a temporary stick to carry the level in the modern loot context. Silk Touch and block entity loot keep their normal entries.
- Emerald gives Resizing for 2,500 power and six capacity. For amplifiers zero through three the scale targets are 0.25, 0.4, 2 and 3. The gradual scale updates modern collision dimensions, eye height and rendering together. It preserves the legacy step, jump and damage rules and denies bed use. Leonard uses the common effect for its size phase and Lilith grows after the summoning entrance.
- Protocol 86 uses `sync_entity_size` to send the current scale when tracking starts, a player logs in, or a player changes dimensions. `WITCResizeScale` saves the in-progress size with the effect. Vanilla effect packets synchronize changes and cleanup for tracking clients.

- In a second muted client, the player shrank from full size to quarter size. The same player traveled to the Nether, passed through a low passage, and kept their size as the observer entered the destination. Clearing the effect restored the model on both clients.
- The final packaged JAR ran both 300- and 600-tick courtships with the bundled Auram development mods. Real beds marked the village after the parents claimed them. Both timed casts produced a child, and both converted zombie parents retained their tags.
- Java 17 data generation and JAR reobfuscation passed. The final focused run passed all nine potion cases with actual bed blocks in the villager fixture. The same run passed eight summoning cases, including both Wither rites that failed in the larger run. Packaged SHA-256 is `b91c5d84e20dc0b8f690ddc85bea58e4d2566418ff2fd9d03abae19a32b8204f`.

### Cauldron Farming and Pulverisation (2026-11-21)

- Dandelion, Bone Meal, Apple, Dirt, and Wheat Seeds add Flowers, Fertilization, Harvesting, Tilling, and Planting. These actions draw the legacy midpoint disk with radius `splashRadius + strength` and preserve the repeated scanlines. Flowers sample the ten original variants with a base one-in-eight chance. Fertilization applies bone meal to the highest plant in the three-layer column. Harvesting removes bushes at any age and emits the loot directly, using Fortune at strength minus one.
- Tilling uses the three-layer scan and requires an empty block above the soil. The latter uses the full splash radius and the log tag, which includes `witchery:witchlog`. Flint adds Pulverisation and uses the original open sphere of radius minus one, with a minimum single-block hit. Stone becomes Cobblestone, Cobblestone becomes Gravel, and Gravel and legacy Sandstone variants become Sand. Both Sand variants are removed and drop one regular Sand item.

Legacy Dirt metadata one and two retain Coarse Dirt and Podzol, while the normal Dirt item is the Tilling ingredient. The stronger repeat produced two and left the water cell. Wolfsbane grew from stage one to seven. Harvesting dropped wheat and stage-four Mandrake Root. Strong Tilling converted supported sand, netherrack, and soul sand. The Planting fixture had six dropped seeds, four new wheat crops, and two loose seeds left. Pruning removed inner leaves and left the outer leaf and log. Felling removed both logs and dropped the Hawthorn log with its wood variant.

Adding Dog Tongue through the original cauldron input triggered the cast. Without a coven, the strengthened Tilling converted sand and left netherrack. ### Cauldron Fluid Holds, Lilify and Dissipate (2026-11-21)

- Sand and Cobblestone hold water and lava for 200 ticks times the duration multiplier. The open sphere uses `distanceSquared < (splashRadius + 2)^2 - 1`. Waterlogged blocks stay intact. The replacement uses `witchery:slurp` with empty collision and a fluid-blocking solid state. Its block entity keeps `HeldFluid` and `RemainingTicks`. Legacy `blockName` distinguishes water and lava, `blockMeta` sets the modern liquid level, and `Timeout` initializes the remaining ticks. A block entity without a valid held fluid clears to air.
- Lily Pad places `witchery:lilypad` above a free water surface in the impact column. Its `growth` state uses strength modulo four. Every 20 ticks, a pad with remaining growth picks one of the twelve original offsets, including the doubled diagonal weights. A successful child has one lower growth, and the parent loses a tier with odds of one in its growth value. Available space, source-water support and Forge protection checks apply to spread. Picking returns a vanilla Lily Pad, and its harvest loot uses the original 25 percent chance.
- Stone clears Witchery gas in the original midpoint disk and radius-high vertical range on either side. Strengthened Dissipate recognizes other Forge gases by fluid density. It deals `(strength + 1) * 5` indirect magic damage to `SpectralUndead`, subject to the creature's damage cap, and leaves ordinary undead alone. Named `minecraft:waterlily` and legacy Red Sand metadata normalize during brew ingredient loading. The brew stays on the ordered `Items` format and needs no new network action.

The second Lily Pad plot was clear of the lava fixture, and both spread pads and the restored water were still present after another dedicated restart.

That rerun covered the legacy familiar fixture and omitted the separate Spectral Familiar ore-search test. ### Cauldron Webs, Vines and Thorns (2026-11-21)

- Thrown Web, distinct from the vanilla Cobweb modifier, retains subtype 56 and adds two levels of Webs for 200 power. Base Webs lays one block at the plantable point. Spiked expands its horizontal contact range by 0.1 blocks per strength level and deals one plus strength cactus damage. Both start from 90 seconds and use the ordinary splash and duration scaling. ### Legacy Save Fixtures and Courtship (2026-11-21)

- An actual Witchery 0.24.1 save from Forge 10.13.4.1614 writes its numeric mapping in the top-level `FML.ItemData` list. Each `K` starts with `0x01` for blocks or `0x02` for items and each `V` holds the numeric ID. Read the mapping from the original save before the modern upgrade rewrites the file.
- The disposable control upgrade lost three Witchery chest stacks and the alder plank block. The oven's old numeric ID turned into light blue concrete. Modern vanilla retained charcoal in the mixed chest and converted the neighboring vanilla planks to Birch.
- The shared Witchery inventory loader now reads legacy `Damage` on the four wood block items after IDs have been resolved. The cauldron delegates its Witchery sapling import to this loader. Existing `BlockStateTag.wood_type` and unrelated tags take precedence.
- In a packaged server, the fixture's numerically resolved Soft Clay Jars, Death Protection Poppet, and Alder Planks loaded through the real oven block entity and survived restart. A scratch chunk overlay preserved the original south-facing oven and inventory, Alder block, all mixed chest stacks, and vanilla Birch at their source coordinates. All eight checks passed before and after restarting. This remains a one-section prototype and a reusable converter is still open.
- The villager courtship diagnostic observed two pending goals finish one tick apart. Forge ticked the second goal after the first set both parents' age cooldowns. Rechecking the parents at birth fixed the duplicate. All nine focused tests passed. The packaged server produced one child for two dosed parents and none when only one had the effect. The saved counts matched after restart.
- The broad suite passed 638 of 640 with the courtship and wood changes and the revised aura fixture. The two failures were the witch rite returning a damaged Arthana and the portable eclipse at night.

### Packaged Legacy World Import (2026-11-21)

- A packaged Forge 47.4.10 server with `-Dwitchery.importLegacyWorld=true --forceUpgrade` captured 121 block and 217 item IDs from the 0.24.1 save before `level.dat` was rewritten. It upgraded the 1,248-chunk copy. The compressed registry is saved as `witchery-legacy-ids.dat` and remains available to later logins.
- At data version 1493 the chunk hook reconstructs oven and witchwood states from the old `Blocks`, `Add`, and `Data` arrays. The real world fixture retained the south-facing oven and clay jars, active oven fuel, alder planks and log axis, persistent hawthorn leaves, alder sapling stage and the alder stair's facing and half. The vanilla chest and its four stacks survived, including Witchery variants, a custom tag, and vanilla charcoal. Vanilla birch remained at its original location.
- A synthetic dedicated player file using the real numeric table logged in with Arthana damage 13, clay jars with a nested Death Protection Poppet and custom tag, vanilla charcoal, and hawthorn planks in the Ender Chest. The packaged client used master volume zero in a background Xvfb session.
- The ordinary packaged restart preserved the south-facing oven and its jar stack, the alder and birch planks, the chest, and all checked player inventory data. All 26 focused Forge GameTests passed in a fresh world. The packaged JAR SHA-256 is `d2b75200df35ca57a58d399dfeaa5dd14d593ae9059333c860e53e32624e3c01`.
- A second fixture built in Forge 10.13.4.1614 exercised the eight crop ages and 14 other block states. The distillery's original ID used the high nibble in `Add`. It retained 791 ticks of progress and the spinning wheel retained 297 after an ordinary dedicated restart. Altar power completed both saved batches and produced two Glowstone Dust, Reek of Misfortune, and a Cobweb.
- The chunk hook applies vanilla item data fixes inside Witchery block entities with inventories outside vanilla's chunk schema. The imported kettle retained the 750 mB tank, RGB `16711935`, a tagged Foul Fume stack and red wool. The poppet shelf retained its tagged Voodoo Poppet and red wool. The saved slot numbers carried through.
- The level-storage hook converts the raw `level.dat` owner tag before vanilla's lazy player upgrade. A synthetic owner record logged into the original world copy in the muted packaged client under Xvfb with OpenAL's No Output device. Save and Quit wrote data version 3465 with the wear, custom tag, wool color and Ender Chest wood species intact. All 28 focused Forge GameTests passed. The packaged JAR SHA-256 is `52851438261ee32b87713a670326be957702c2dfb1f3619d09ddff4367512374`.
- This workflow uses disposable copies. The remaining migration includes unsupported terrain and block entities, specialized entity fields, and legacy dimension folders. The modern poppet shelf uses a fixed orientation.

### Legacy Saved Entities (2026-11-21)

- The fresh 31-case combined regression passed 26 cases. The failures were Reflection loot, the Sprouting lift, protected tree rollback, the giant trunk base, and powered bolt effect removal. ### Legacy Spirit Portals and Dream Fluids (2026-11-21)

### Spreading Herbs and Spanish Moss (2026-11-22)

- Glint Weed and Ember Moss share the original one-in-six growth attempt and four-step walk. They spread on grass, dirt variants, mycelium, farmland, and both sand colors. The fifth nearby plant stops further spreading. Glint Weed can survive below a solid block and gives 14 light. Ember Moss gives six light and burns a living contact for three seconds, with creative players and fire-immune entities exempt.

Decorative saves from the earlier port acquire a nearby support when available.

All ten states matched the original region data and survived an ordinary dedicated restart. The imported Ember Moss set a pig on fire. Shears yielded one of either moss, hand harvest yielded zero, and Glint Weed dropped normally. Removing the south support cleared the upper plant and its dependent strand.

### Brew Buckets and Stored Fluids (2026-11-22)

- An empty bucket uses Forge's fluid interaction path to collect 1,000 mB of a cauldron brew. The registered Brew fluid type makes the filled item with a copy of the mixture tag. The filled bucket exposes the same ordered inputs and color to a cauldron or Forge fluid handler and returns an empty bucket when emptied.
- The cauldron exposes the effective brew separately from its saved liquid volume. A fill can resume or combine matching ordered ingredients and matching visible color. It keeps the legacy base color on a restored brew and rejects unrelated fluids and reordered ingredients. A simulated transfer changes neither tank.
- Original Forge assembled the Nether Wart and Sugar brew, drained one bucket through the registered fluid container, and saved the two remaining buckets in the cauldron. The packaged port loaded the chest item and the cauldron with the original color and order. It also recovered six source, nearby, and falling Brew and Disease cells.
- Classic Brew used two quanta and a seven-tick interval. Disease had one quantum and fifteen ticks. The importer translates the original pending updates into Forge source or flowing fluid ticks. Disease additionally gets a block tick because modern flow and random decay run separately. The raw regions matched ten flow updates and two decay updates.
- The packaged headless server reopened the same chest, cauldron, and six fluid cells. A pig entered the imported Disease fluid and received the Diseased effect. The fixture turned off Disease decay to hold the cells for this check.
- Java 17 data generation and the reobfuscated JAR succeeded. All 41 required GameTests passed, including the bucket interaction, cold refill, simulated transfer, ordered merge, and save of the Swiftness brew. The final JAR SHA-256 is `be01cfd05575f79863752cd8c24928d4ec43c451328da612e8a64c36c66f447f`. The Brew Bucket texture remains to be checked live.

### Cauldron Quicklime and Gypsum (2026-11-22)

- The normal item drop into the Witch's Cauldron now checks Quicklime and Gypsum before heat and ritual restrictions. Gypsum removes the whole dropped stack and clears the tank. Quicklime requires a prepared brew and a player within the item's original five-block expanded bounding box. Its block-safe explosion starts one and a half blocks above the cauldron's base and excludes the nearest player from damage and knockback.
- Brew effects, modifiers, and fixed ritual offerings add half a point each to the power of one, with a maximum of ten. The active fixed recipe ID accounts for its offered trigger. Dye alone leaves a power-one brew. Quicklime stays intact in an empty or plain cauldron, and in a prepared brew with no nearby player.
- An intermittent Alluring Skull GameTest failure showed that the zombie disappeared before its first tick. A distant player from another GameTest can trigger vanilla distance despawning. The test marks its manually created mobs persistent, matching the normal GameTest spawn helper while retaining their disabled AI goals and normal navigation.
- All 47 required GameTests passed, alongside Java 17 data generation and JAR reobfuscation. The new cases check blast reach, player exclusion, terrain protection, dye-only and plain liquids, saved tank state, and interruption of the original pork cooking ritual.
- The packaged headless server used a disposable copy of the previously imported Nether Wart and Sugar brew. Four Quicklime rested in the vessel with its original 2,000 mB intact. Three dropped Gypsum cleared it and left all four Quicklime, which were also present after save and restart. The packaged SHA-256 is `54f937b9c00563b041eaf1f5ebb8350c3589059bb91f46b728a3f8461835f77a`.

### Fixed Cauldron Misfortune (2026-11-22)

### Blood Magic Compatibility (2026-11-22)

- The four diagnostic Mutandis reruns each produced and registered all six items at the position of the previous intermittent failure. The fixture and production cauldron code are unchanged. The failure remains unexplained.
- Auram's Blood Magic 3.3.5-47 provides the UUID-backed Soul Network and Sacrifice Knife event. The integration keeps the original Life Essence thresholds and works even when the target has no Witchery infusion. A direct Drain Magic brew took 20,000 Life Essence to 13,750. A powered Anti-Magic Bolt applied the half-tier debit on impact and the full-tier debit to its shooter. Carnosa Diem added 200 after its two-point sacrifice. [Blood Magic 3.3.5-47](https://www.curseforge.com/minecraft/mc-mods/blood-magic/files/7253333)
- Using Blood Magic's actual Sacrificial Dagger filled its altar and moved a vampire's blood power from 250 to 50 with health still at 20. Another use left the altar and player unchanged. A regular player continued to pay with health. The hook spends blood only if the event still allows the altar to fill.
- All 64 GameTests passed in the disposable Blood Magic and Patchouli profile, including two integration fixtures. All 62 checked-in cases passed on clean Forge. Java 17 data generation, JAR reobfuscation, and both packaged headless boots passed. The JAR SHA-256 is `23976ec7cf72f802ed915a564ff241badc5412f312769a0cb44511968efa9be5`.

### Thaumaturge Wands and Aspects (2026-11-22)

- Witchery's legacy drain loop attempts one more 100-centivis payment than its countdown value. Thaumcraft 4.2.3.5 tries the equipped Vis Amulet first, then scans the main inventory from its highest slot to the hotbar. The modern amulet recharges wands directly. The compatibility hook runs through Thaumaturge's wand API and preserves available whole payments, per-aspect limits, wand modifiers, and inventory order. [Thaumcraft 4.2.3.5](https://www.curseforge.com/minecraft/mc-mods/thaumcraft/files/2227552)
- The packaged data maps use the existing Thaumaturge aspect definitions. Legacy aspects with removed IDs use their closest supported aspect and sum when two names map to the same supported ID. For example, Arbor and Herba map to Herba, Fames maps to Desiderium, and Limus maps to Victus. Item IDs for ordinary registered subtypes and all 31 original entity types retain their own entries. Thaumaturge adds its normal equipment-derived aspects when it builds the live item index.
- Thaumaturge originally applied its generic leaves tag after Witchery's explicit entry. Declaring optional Forge load order makes base tags load first. The final runtime check compared every base item and entity entry, checked Condensed Fear through the live item index, and verified the existing vanilla apple and cow entries.
- An incremental Java compile dropped the production Mixin reference map. Main Java compilation now processes the full source set when it runs and declares the generated reference and remapping files as outputs. Removing the generated reference map staged for the JAR and running `jar` rebuilt the identical map and included it in the reobfuscated artifact. The same JAR booted headlessly with Thaumaturge and on clean Forge. The SHA-256 is `ec0f3b364899e4acc4905dc97b9e9639a0c56510c06309473ec8d5a62f840eae`.

### Staff of Duplication (2026-11-22)

### Atlas and Journal Navigation (2026-11-22)

- The 14 atlas biome families follow the order in the original `ItemBook.BIOME_TYPES`. The modern index records each nonempty family's first page and adds a final Other biomes entry when available. Contents can switch between family shortcuts and individual biome pages.
- Protocol 87 adds the displayed biome's ID to atlas bookmark messages. The server resolves it in its current tag order and uses the page hint to preserve the selected occurrence when a biome appears in several families. Saved IDs remain stable across reordering. If the saved ID disappears, the atlas cannot silently copy a different biome into a note.
- The original journal advances through the same sequence of 16 pages. The Cover control returns to the first page. Closing the journal saves the complete `pageStack` for the requested unlocked chapter. Earlier port stacks with a single bookmark still open at that chapter. Torn-page upgrades preserve the trail and journal progress.
- Java 17 data generation and reobfuscation succeeded. All 73 clean GameTests and 77 optional GameTests passed. A packaged dedicated fixture saved Snowy Beach and the journal's 14-entry trail, then reopened the same player after a full restart with a datapack moving Snowy Beach out of the beach tag. It crafted the same biome note, restored the ninth torn page, and cleared the trail at the cover while preserving all nine torn pages. Packaged language keys were present. The Docker daemon did not answer the isolated client environment check. The packaged JAR SHA-256 is `eb26440d4530693a064227baaac97b8dd574bdbac28302d22202f771565a26f4`.

### Coffin and Vampire Workshop Import (2026-11-21)

- Legacy Coffins use the old bed direction, part, and occupancy bits. Read each lid's `Opened` field into the flattened palette before chunk post-processing loads the block entities. In the first packaged run, the open world state and the tile's persisted value disagreed when the tile tried to open itself during that load. Including the lid in the palette state preserves both representations. Matched halves reconnect after neighboring chunks load, and the foot takes its occupancy from the head.

Disabling only that addon in the test copy reached texture loading. Docker emitted an `oom` event and the process exited 137 at its 6 GiB limit. A 9 GiB retry initialized the No Output audio device and was killed creating the 16,384 by 16,384 block atlas with four mip levels. The next isolated launch uses zero mip levels, a 6 GiB Java heap, and a 10 GiB container limit. [Evolved Mekanism 1.3.0-fix3 notes](https://www.curseforge.com/minecraft/mc-mods/evolved-mekanism/files/9685752)
- The pack's resource reload found the crossbow `random.click` and `random.wind_up` recordings missing. Both OGGs exist in the supplied legacy assets. A direct check of the modern sound definitions found these two unresolvable Witchery files.
- The 10 GiB retry at zero mip levels initialized OpenAL on No Output and passed the full block atlas. It created smaller atlases and was then killed by Docker during resource loading. The isolated profile had the AuramBSL shader enabled on this run. The subsequent isolated retry disables shaders and uses the new sounds and stairs JAR.
- The muted, shader-free retry with the sounds and stairs JAR reached the 634-mod title screen. Opening the fresh-world screen reached datapack and recipe preparation, then Docker logged an out-of-memory event and exit 137. The isolated profile used a 6 GiB Java heap and 10 GiB container limit. No new world was saved.

The first attempt failed because Draconic Evolution Render Patcher rejects dedicated servers. The next ordinary restart retained the Ash in result slot 2 and both Hopper tile records. The cause of the first missing records is still open.

### Cauldron Inferno, Hell Gate, and Blast (2026-11-21)

- Glint Weed adds Inferno for three effect levels and 750 power. Block casts launch `3 + strength` droplets from the struck side. Direct creature casts ignite for `2 + 2 * strength` seconds and full exposure launches the same burst. Glancing creature hits have no effect.

It respects Forge placement protection, ignites struck creatures for five seconds, and splits at levels above one on block impact. Original `Level`, `Color`, and `Effect` data reload with the vanilla projectile position, motion and owner. Older scaffold saves retain the corresponding spawn-egg item ID.
- Refined Evil subtype 38 adds Hell Gate for five levels and 3,000 power. Direct creature casts give `witchery:potion.hellishaura` for three minutes before scaling. The effect pulses every `25 >> amplifier` ticks, to a maximum frequency of once per tick. The host deals one point of armor-bypassing vanilla fire damage to adjacent creatures within its horizontally expanded bounds and lights them for strength seconds. The tracked effect renders as original green fire. Infernal circle validation uses the medium 24-glyph cauldron shape at the first Waystone, or at the cauldron if the brew has no Waystone. Only an instant block ritual attempts to summon a Demon.
- Gold Ingot adds Blast for five levels and 500 power. Its vanilla explosion occurs at the adjacent block face or the creature's current position. Full exposure always triggers it, and other exposure uses `0.2 * exposure`. Mojang 1.7.10's `World.createExplosion` delegates with flaming disabled and terrain damage enabled for this call. The modern cast uses the standard Forge-visible destructive explosion and brew strength as the radius.
- The instant ritual now passes a positive pulse index separately from ordinary and ritual-rain impacts. This routes Hell Gate and Transpose Ore through their ritual terrain effects without changing ordinary droplet behavior.

The Hellish Aura host kept its visible green overlay with potion bubbles disabled and the adjacent pig burned. The distant pig stayed at full health.
- A Waystone, Refined Evil mixture, and Dog Tongue saved a Hell Gate ritual at warmup pulse one. The headless restart retained the aura, Blast crater and fire, and the ritual spawned a Demon inside the Infernal circle. ### Blood Goblet and Lilith (2026-11-20)

- Three bottles and a glass block craft `witchery:glassgoblet`. An equipped Boline and a chicken at the original skull, tripwire, torch and redstone arrangement fill the hotbar goblet. Its original `WITCBloodUUID` and `WITCBloodName` distinguish `__chicken`, `__lilith` and player sources. The filled goblet keeps its original item ID and uses a model predicate. The muted client crafted it, filled it in survival, attempted the ritual during daytime and then succeeded at night with the same items.
- `witchery:follower` type zero is Elle. She follows her summoner until she finds a radius-six lava surface with clear space around its center. The original footprint checks surface sources. Once over it, she gives the first warning at 200 ticks and becomes Lilith at 400. The original follower and boss registry IDs, appearances, dialogue and sounds are used.
- Lilith has 200 health, caps damage at 12 per hit and heals five per second. Weakness or Chilled reduces recovery to one per second. A reflected large fireball halts recovery for ten seconds. She attacks with fireballs and the original five symbols. The muted client used a supplied enchanted sword, a splash potion and test resistance in the disposable arena. Defeat left a friendly Lilith in the world. She returned the client's actual goblet filled with her blood.
- The muted client drank that goblet, reached rank one with 125 blood power, retained the empty item and displayed the original droplet meter. The capability and full player-data sync carry legacy `VampireLevel`, `VampireLevelCap`, `BloodPower` and `HumanBlood`. Protocol 25 includes the restored entity data. The player placed a coffin in shade, slept at tick 6,095 and woke at dusk with all 125 blood power. All 140 required Forge GameTests passed, along with data generation and JAR reobfuscation.
- The dedicated restart preserved rank one, 125 blood power, the empty goblet, and the open coffin. The original meter returned on rejoin. Noon beneath the roof kept all 125 blood power. The first sunlight exposure outside the roof drained it to zero and ignited the player.

### Vampire Feeding and Mesmerize (2026-11-20)

- Scrolling past the first hotbar slot selects the fangs and then the eye, where unlocked. The target's blood droplets appear below the crosshair. `hudShowVampireTargetBloodText` optionally shows the numeric value. Protocol 26 syncs the selected power and limits target blood queries to visible nearby entities. The selected power resets on rank advancement and rejoin.
- The muted survival client used the same vampire from the Lilith test. It reached rank two by feeding to 750. Mesmerize spent 50 blood and applied six seconds of paralysis. Feeding at roughly 1.65 blocks transferred ten blood from the paralyzed villager. The client used sneak-use in the air to activate vision. Five GameTests checked the bite range, animal cap, PvP rules, feeding trial, vision, paralysis and sleep recovery.
- The muted client completed six careful sips across two villagers and reached rank three. The packaged dedicated restart kept rank three, 1,000 blood power, vision and both villagers' 262 blood. Vanilla villagers save blood under `ForgeData.WitcheryExtendedVillager.Blood`, and player data uses the original `VampireQuestCounter` and `VampireVision` tags. All 145 required Forge GameTests passed with data generation and JAR reobfuscation.

### Observations of an Immortal and Rank Four (2026-11-20)

- `witchery:vampirebook` uses the original item ID, book icon, parchment, diagrams, chapter order and Torn Page requirements. It uses new, concise English guidance for the original tasks. The book's base recipe is six nether wart, a Nether Star, a book and garlic. One Torn Page crafts onto an unfinished book and preserves its bookmark and name. Legacy ingredient damage 160 maps to `witchery:torn_vampire_page`. Legacy book damage 0–9 maps to `VampireBookPages`. The `pageStack` paths still load their last chapter.
- Bosses drop a page when killed by a player carrying an unfinished book. Zombified Piglins, Endermen, villagers and undead also use the original chances. In the muted dedicated client, three actual kills of weakened Withers produced pages. The player crafted each into the same book.
- Protocol 27 validates a chapter bookmark from the held book when closed. The saved `VampireLevelCap` only increases. A base book raised the cap to three and the book with three restored pages raised it to four. The muted client tested the regular survival craft, page turns, collecting the boss drops and all three upgrades. A crouching empty-hand strike moved a pig six blocks.
- The dedicated trial accumulated one interval every 40 player ticks at night. Daytime under a roof paused it at 158 intervals. The server restart retained the book's three pages and speed bookmark, the rank cap and the elapsed dark time. The same rank-three character completed 300 intervals through normal survival ticks, reached rank four, and used speed twice for a total of 20 blood. The resulting Speed IV and Jump Boost III refused a third stack at rank four. The original diagrams render on a single page with arrow and keyboard navigation. Data generation, JAR reobfuscation and all 151 required GameTests passed.

### Village Keeps and Watchtowers (2026-11-21)

- The keep and watchtower templates follow `ComponentVillageKeep` and `ComponentVillageWatchTower`, including their ladders, guard platforms, and separate loot tables. The five inhabited village styles use road jigsaws. The desert and snowy processor lists follow the legacy `GetVillageBlockID` and `GetVillageBlockMeta` terrain hooks. The keep's courtyard and rampart have four and two persistent guards, respectively. The watchtower has four at its lookout. Fixed guard entries preserve the original totals without depending on chunk order.
- `townKeepMode` and `townKeepWeight` restore the keep settings. Each watchtower cluster has its own pool element and seeded quota. The vanilla codec allows up to 150 copies per raw entry, so larger weights use several entries referencing the same quota. The quota for each cluster is consumed by placements from that element alone.

The village at the same coordinates still generated with neither addition.

It walked into the keep at `642 63 847` and used assisted positioning for a golden helmet from the roof chamber. The disposable GameTest world used for the final pass was fresh because the reusable world still contained guards from a previous run.

### Sky’s Wrath and Total Eclipse (2026-11-21)

- Legacy rite IDs 14–18 have five recipes that each use a small white circle. The three storm variants use an unworn wooden, stone, or iron sword and Wood Ash. The first two cost 1,000 or 2,000 power and accept a same-dimension location-bound Waystone. The portable variant uses a Charged Attuned Stone. Legacy axe and Quicklime offerings distinguish the two eclipse recipes.
- The Heart Glyph persists `Storm.Target`, `Storm.Greater`, `Storm.Stage`, `StepTicks` and its consumed offerings. The first four 30-tick phases each spawn one bolt except the third, which spawns two. The fourth starts a five- to fifteen-minute thunderstorm. The greater storm keeps the original offsets on both axes. Short-lived chunk tickets keep remote lightning active. A manual cancellation returns the offerings and retains the altar debit.
- Total Eclipse advances the current day to midnight. Weather flags and the clock use the shared 1.20.1 Overworld data. `riteOfEclipseCooldownInSecs` defaults to zero and accepts up to 3,600 seconds. When enabled, the cooldown follows the original per-dimension session lifetime and only applies with multiple players in that dimension.
- A native `--nogui` server recorded the greater storm's first strike at `(775, 242, 856)` for a Waystone bound to `(770, 242, 850)`. A restart loaded phase one and the same target, followed by a saved thunderstorm. The muted client also activated the focused and portable storms, rejected a night eclipse, and advanced days two and three to midnight with the portable and powered recipes. Two muted clients verified that a 60-second cooldown returned the axe, Quicklime and stone and preserved noon. Protocol 71 carries the weather recipe actions.
- The release JAR preserved all three portable storm offerings when the muted player activated a prepared Dream World circle.
- Circle Magic rendered the new focused storm and eclipse pages on the reconnected client. Java 17 data generation, JAR reobfuscation, and all 397 required Forge GameTests passed. The six new cases cover recipe synchronization, the Dream restriction, lightning positions and existing thunder, partial phase saves, remote range and refunds, day gating, and multiplayer cooldown. The production JAR SHA-256 is `21c26bc4b6e684ee57c73d4e879b278f6392f27cf2fd2c70c4311897dad20287`.

### Protection, Imprisonment and Barrier Rites (2026-11-21)

- Rites 8–12 restore four-block creature repulsion and confinement plus the focused, greater and portable barriers. Powered versions use an altar in range of the Heart Glyph for activation and upkeep. Protection and Imprisonment draw 0.8 power per tick, focused Barrier draws 1.2, and greater Barrier draws 1.4. Imprisonment preserves the original edge prediction and the legacy rotation.
- The focused barrier uses radius four and a five-block wall. Greater and portable barriers use radius six and six- or four-block walls. The midpoint-circle pattern places the floor below the target and the roof above the wall. Powered variants accept optional location-bound Waystones, with the same reach as existing landscape rites. A refresh gives each cell 30 ticks, and the portable rite completes on the sixtieth one-second phase. Barrier block entities send collision changes to clients and retain their lifetime in saves.

### Queued Circle Rites (2026-11-21)

- One Heart Glyph activation matches the item rites by original registration ID. Each match reserves its physical offering sources in memory, including the legacy additional matching Grassper when a drop satisfies the ingredient. The heart pulls actual offerings during each cast. A missing offering returns the current cast's staged items and starts the next recipe. A second activation clears the whole queue and all ongoing upkeep.
- `QueuedRites` saves the remaining recipe IDs in order. `Rite`, `StepTicks`, `Sacrifices`, and the initiating player's UUID retain the active cast. `ProtectionRites` and `UpkeepRites` store independent upkeep ordered by `UpkeepOrder`. When the foreground is idle, the first timed rite keeps its earlier `Landscape`, `OreDepth`, or `Recharging` fields at the root. Supported 1.7.10 upkeep entries can all migrate together, including infusion recharge with stage zero. A partial Forge-era pulse in a migrated root tag carries over to its first timed rite.

### Cauldron Dispersal and Waystone Targets (2026-11-20)

- The 20-pulse warmup now feeds all four thrown dispersal modes. Each active pulse charges the altar before acting. Gas follows the surface up to seven blocks above or below the target elevation and expands for eight pulses plus eight per extent level. Liquid rains for ten pulses plus five per lifetime level. ### Witch's Hand and Otherwhere Powers (2026-11-20)

- Protocol 14 sends authoritative infusion charge on login, respawn, grant, recharge and use. The meter uses Snow, Dirt, Nether Portal and Netherrack, with left-side placement by default. - The Hand supports Otherwhere blink for one charge, point recall for two, lifting the caster and a creature for two, and companion recall for four. Hold use while sneaking for three seconds to remember the position, then release. Short sneak use recalls the player, and sneak attack recalls with the attacked creature. It keeps `WITCRecallD`, `WITCRecallX`, `WITCRecallY` and `WITCRecallZ` in persisted player data, and uses `WITCCooldown` on the item. Vanilla recall dimensions preserve their numeric values, and modded dimensions use resource IDs. Three special dimensions block recall. Ender Inhibition III prevents the affected participant from teleporting.
- All 52 required Forge GameTests passed, including legacy recall coordinates and cooldown, blink, lift and separate Arthana drop chance coverage. Data generation and the reobfuscated JAR succeeded.
- The packaged clean Forge test used the naturally infused Otherwhere player and the Hand collected from a witch. The four successful actions spent 1, 2, 2 and 4 charge. The dedicated server saw the caster and pig return from the Nether to X 123.5, Y 110, Z 32.5 in the Overworld with 191 charge, and the Hand, both entities, recall fields and charge were confirmed on reconnect after a dedicated restart. Ender Inhibition III stopped recall and preserved charge. Lowering only the scratch client's graphics settings restored responsive controls after a previous client timeout.

### Light Infusion Hand Powers (2026-11-20)

- The Light mode cloaks after 30 ticks and refreshes every 30 ticks for one charge. Its barrier shield and cage use the original translucent sprite and disappear after 200 loaded ticks. Short sneak use costs three, and an attack that raises a creature costs five. A barrier remembers the owner by UUID and reads legacy name-only saves. Other survival players and mobs collide with it.
- The clean muted client performed the Light rite again with its second bottled Ghost of the Light before using the Witch's Hand. It spent two charge while cloaked. Releasing ended Invisibility. It rendered and walked through its three-by-three shield, watched it expire, raised a pig four blocks into a cage, caged a second pig in place, and projected 15 blocks from a stone face. The server verified barrier ownership and charge 200 to 184 for those five actions.
- All 55 required Forge GameTests passed. Targeted coverage includes clearing a zombie's recent aggression, canceling the cloak while preserving ordinary Invisibility, shield collision and expiry, legacy ownership and a blocked raised cage. Data generation and JAR reobfuscation passed.

### Overworld Infusion Hand Powers (2026-11-20)

- The Overworld Hand uses two charge to disarm a mob within four blocks, two or four to repel metal equipment, three to launch stone from an exposed side, and two to lift a supported terrain column. Hold for two seconds while looking clear to charge a shockwave at six charge per second. The soft-landing power costs five, or ten for a sneaking impact explosion.
- A short sneak-use at an ore costs two and produces two ingots. After 40 ticks of sneak-channeling, a magnet pulse costs one every four ticks and each ore converted during the pulse costs two. Use Forge ore and ingot tags in place of the Ore Dictionary and preserve the surrounding stone type for deepslate and Nether ores. Legacy ingredient damage 55 migrates to `witchery:rock`; the projectile deals six damage. The Java 17 build and all 60 required GameTests passed.
- The muted survival client performed the Overworld rite with the bottled brew and bound poppet from the fixture. It collected two targeted gold ingots and drew in four iron ingots including one from the channeled ore. A zombie dropped its sword, its chainmail accepted the upward shove, and a rock took a pig from ten health to four. A raised column moved terracotta three blocks and preserved the chest. A two-second wave took another pig from ten to two. Both soft landings preserved ten health. The sneak landing spent ten charge and blew open the snow and underlying stone, leaving 151 charge. A dedicated restart preserved the charge, Hand, raised terracotta and adjacent chest.

### Poppet Shelf (2026-11-20)

Forge loaded the shelf before anyone joined.

### Specialized Protection Poppets (2026-11-20)

- Restore legacy Earth (1), Water (2), Hunger (4), Tool (5) and Armor (11) poppet variants, original shaped recipes and single-Taglock binding. They work in player inventory and on shelves, with inventory first. Earth protects from lethal falls and falling blocks without healing. Water protects from lethal drowning and adds three seconds of Water Breathing. Hunger protects when starvation would leave two health or less and adds three seconds of Saturation. The Death ward's fallback effects last six seconds.
- Tool repair runs on player interaction, and armor repair runs on injury. Eligible equipment at the original 90% wear threshold gets a full repair. A bound ward is consumed when used. All 72 required Forge GameTests passed, and data generation and reobfuscation succeeded.
- The muted survival client crafted and personally Taglock-bound the Earth ward. The test supplied already-bound Water, Hunger, Tool and Armor wards. Dedicated damage commands verified unhealed Earth protection, Water Breathing and Saturation. A real stone block break brought a pickaxe from 235 wear to one, and an incoming hit brought an iron chestplate from 222 wear to zero. A full dedicated restart kept both repairs and the equipment wards remained consumed.

  - fix: in `/Users/cyberpwn/development/workspace/AuramMods/Witchery/src/main/java/art/arcane/witchery/registry/WitcheryBlocks.java`, `LegacyNonSolidShapeBlock` now applies `.dynamicShape()` when `hasCollision=true`.
  - verification:
    - `./gradlew compileJava` passes.
    - `./gradlew runClient` launch smoke reached full registry freeze/atlas initialization without the previous `plantmine` exception (manual stop after startup).
- Guardrail: if any fluid ID is added/renamed, update `LEGACY_FLUID_CONFIGS` immediately or startup will fail fast with `Missing fluid config for legacy fluid`.

## Critical Architecture Facts
- Legacy bootstrap class: `com.emoniph.witchery.Witchery`.
- Lifecycle pattern:
  - `preInit`: config, worldgen setup, villages, packet preInit, construct Potions/Fluids/Blocks/Items/Entities, recipe+potion preInit.
  - `init`: packet init, entity init, GUI handler, dimension registration, seeds, proxy hooks, mod hooks init, potions init, recipes init, brew registry init.
  - `postInit`: recipes postInit, mod hooks postInit, biome integration, event registration, dispenser behaviors, chunk loading callback.
  - `serverLoad`: server command + runtime registries (`PowerSources`, `AreaMarkerRegistry`, worldgen initiate).

## High-Risk Porting Points
- 1.7 code uses side-effect registration in overridden name setters:
  - `BlockBase.func_149663_c` and `BlockBaseContainer.func_149663_c` register blocks.
  - `ItemBase.func_77655_b` registers items.
- Many game objects are metadata variants under `ItemGeneral` (not separate item registries).
- Block entities are often auto-registered through `BlockBaseContainer` using block names.
- Several systems depend on custom singleton registries (rites, predictions, infusions, creature powers, symbol effects).
- Legacy dimension registration uses `DimensionManager.registerProviderType/registerDimension` and must be redesigned for modern dimension keys + datapack dimensions.
